# Credits

The pack does not redistribute mods: packwiz downloads them from their official source (Modrinth, CurseForge or the author's Maven repository). Each mod keeps its licence.

## Base

| What | Author | Licence | Use |
|---|---|---|---|
| GregTech CEu Modern 7.5.3 | GregTechCEu | LGPL-3.0 | Base mod. GTM casings and textures are referenced, not copied. |
| KubeJS, Rhino | Latvian / KubeJS-Mods | LGPL-3.0 | Pack scripts. |
| FTB Quests, Library, Teams | FTB | Author's licence | Quest book. |
| Wildcard Pattern 0.1.2 | LeoDreamer (github.com/LeoDreamer2004/Wildcard-Pattern) | MIT | Mod from its GitHub release; our assembler recipe. |
| Gregtech Terminals 6.8.5 (gtceuterminal) | Bgame (github.com/BgameXl/gtceu-terminal-addon) | MIT | Installed mod. The Hologram Projector calls its public `BlockPatternReflection` to read GTM's pattern grid; no code copied. |

## Ideas and processes

Ideas are not copied as text or as code: they are rebuilt with our rules, and we say where they come from.

| Idea in Gregtech Grindless | Where it comes from | Source licence | What we take |
|---|---|---|---|
| The Mk1 as a materials machine: no transmutation into osmium or uranium, duranium only from the Mk2, and the research chain made explicit | GTM 7.5.3's own fusion recipes, `FusionReactorMachine` (start capacity, LuV voltage cap, fusion overclock) and research chain, read from the pack's dump and GTM's source; GTCA's calcium plasma used by id | LGPL-3.0 (GTM, GTCA; referenced) | The audit and the cuts are ours (`tables/fusion_mk1.json`, `luv/fusion.js`). GTM's duranium recipe is re-registered with its own id, fluids and figures and a 200 M EU start. No external code copied. |
| Zero-g crystal grower, rotating molecular farm and organoid lab | Skylab/ISS crystal-growth experiments and KAGRA sapphire mirrors; ORF Genetics' growth factors in barley, CollPlant's recombinant collagen in tobacco; ISS organoid research and FinalSpark's living-neuron computing | Real science and engineering | Concepts only. Original patterns, recipes and controller pixel art. The annular farm's docking controller is on its near rim because the shared pattern helper requires a front-slice controller; its bearing hub remains central. |
| Microgravity smelter: ZBLAN optical fibre, metal-foam Whipple shields and containerless alloying; wake-shield epitaxy and multi-junction wafers | Poulain and Lucas's ZBLAN (1975), Flawless Photonics on the ISS (2024), ESA FOAM and the ISS electromagnetic levitator; NASA/University of Houston Wake Shield Facility (STS-60/69/80) | Real engineering; GTM LGPL-3.0 APIs referenced | Concepts only. Original structures, scripts and controller art; no external code or artwork copied. GTM's material-info removal API prevents optical-pipe recycling from being regenerated. |
| Orbital solar station: two multi-junction photovoltaic wings feeding ordinary or wireless dynamos | Glaser's solar power satellite (1968), NASA/DOE reference system (1979), Caltech MAPLE (2023); GCYR's photovoltaic cell used by id; the pack's proven lunar array and GTM generator helper | Real engineering / LGPL-3.0 (GCYR and GTM, referenced) | The concept; original structure and scripts. No external code or art copied. The original controller face is generated by `tools/assets/Build-OrbitalSolarTextures.ps1`. |
| Fumarole condenser (Io): volcanic gases cooled in bands of tungstensteel, stainless steel, aluminium and frostproof zones over a fumarole field; germanium and indium from the sublimate (GeCl₄ distilled with an HCl charge that is kept, the oxide reduced with hydrogen); sulfur concrete cast from sulfur and basalt | Real: the rhenium, indium and germanium sublimates of Kudryavy volcano (Kuril Islands; Korzhinsky et al., 1994) and the volatile metals of fumaroles; sulfur concrete for in-situ building on the Moon and Mars (NASA MSFC studies); Biomes O' Plenty's brimstone fumarole and black sand used by id | Real science / BOP (by id, nothing copied) | The concept and the bands; Io's sublimates are by analogy. Structure, figures, code and the controller face (`tools/assets/Build-IoSurfaceTextures.ps1`) are ours. |
| Volcanic intake (Io): a closed Rankine loop of sulfur dioxide between a patera's magma and Io's 110 K sky, up to four turbine modules, a wearing heat-exchanger bundle; radiation vault casings on every Jovian machine (graded-Z titanium then tantalum) | Real: Io's heat flow (Veeder and others; Galileo and Juno), Loki Patera; SO₂ studied as a Rankine working fluid; Juno's titanium electronics vault; the pack's Venus heat engine and GTM generator helper | Real science / GTM LGPL-3.0 APIs referenced | The concept; original structure and scripts. No external code or art copied. The controller face is generated by `tools/assets/Build-IoSurfaceTextures.ps1`. |
| Flux-tube tether (Io orbit, LuV B7): a bare electrodynamic tether paid out from a station into Io's plasma torus, two reels of spooled aluminium tape and aramid with a Whipple-shielded end mass, a hollow-cathode argon port; power from Jupiter's spin with no reboost | Real: Sanmartín, Lorenzini and others, *Bare-tether power generation and propulsion at Jupiter* (2005–2008); the Io flux tube (Bigg, 1964; Voyager); the pack's GTM generator helper | Real science / GTM LGPL-3.0 APIs referenced | The concept; original structure and scripts. No external code or art copied. The controller face is generated by `tools/assets/Build-IoOrbitTextures.ps1`. |
| Sulfur-iodine hydrogen plant (Io, LuV B7's fallback): the thermochemical sulfur-iodine cycle on a patera's heat (sulfuric acid decomposition, the Bunsen reaction, HI splitting) with kept acid and iodine charges, up to four decomposer modules | Real: General Atomics' sulfur-iodine cycle (1970s); JAEA's continuous 150-hour run (2019) | Real science | The concept; original structure and scripts. No external code or art copied. The controller face is generated by `tools/assets/Build-IoOrbitTextures.ps1`. |

| The sunlight table: one factor per place, new airless places at the Moon's ×1.4 over the square of the distance in AU | Inverse-square law; the pack's own EV and IV solar machines | Public knowledge / original | Our table (`tables/sunlight.json`), keeping the as-built EV and IV factors. |

## Quests

No literal text has been copied from any book. Structure and task design conventions have been adapted.

| What | Where it comes from | Licence | What we take |
|---|---|---|---|
| One quest per machine; optional sibling tasks for LP/HP; gear shape only for key multiblocks | Star Technology, `config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/early_game.snbt` | MIT | Structure conventions |
| Base options from `data.snbt`, a named chapter group, a small reward per quest | Monifactory, `config/ftbquests/quests/data.snbt`, `chapter_groups.snbt`, `chapters/groundwork.snbt` | LGPL-3.0 | Structure and option conventions (derived file) |
| Cross-check of GTM IDs | TerraFirmaGreg | No licence | Reference only |
| Applied Energistics tab: component ladder 4k–256k, terminals → patterns → provider → CPU, quests for ExtendedAE, Expanded AE, MEGA and the GT ME hatches | Star Technology, `config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/applied_energistics.snbt` and its `kubejs/assets/ftbquestlocalizer/lang/en_us.json` | MIT | Structure and quest selection; new texts with our recipes |
| Workshop and Storage tabs: one central node with a branch per mod; the GT Terminal that builds multiblocks | Star Technology, `config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/quality_of_life.snbt` | MIT | Structure and quest idea |
| Short AE2 and storage texts (channels per controller face, energy acceptor, smart and dense cables, interface, terminals, P2P, controller and drawer keys, Sophisticated upgrades) | Reclamation (ACCBDD/reclamation-dev), `.minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/digital_storage.snbt` and `kubejs/assets/ftbquestlocalizer/lang/en_us.json` | MIT | Adapted texts, not literal |
| AE2 texts with GT: channels, crafting CPU (storage versus co-processors), buses, cards, P2P that neither pushes nor pulls, ME Output/Stocking Bus and Pattern Buffer | Cosmic Frontiers (Frontiers-PackForge/CosmicFrontiers, branch 0.7.3), `overrides/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/digital_roots_ae2_basic.snbt` | LGPL-3.0 | Ideas and adapted texts, not literal |
| Pattern Provider that receives the machine's output, Programmed Circuit Card in the provider, Pattern Modifier for parallel multiblocks | Quantum Skies (jmoiron/quantum-skies), `pack/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/digital.snbt` | LGPL-2.1 | Ideas and adapted texts, not literal |
| Travel and Teams tab: Waystones and hang glider | ReFactory (VeljkoBogdan/ReFactory), `config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/transportation.snbt` | MIT | Structure |
| Productive Bees tab: nests, cage, hive, upgrades and centrifuge as a quest chain | ValineServer1Expert (starMaylight), `config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/productivebees.snbt` | MIT | Structure (the original has no English text) |
| Welcome and Steam: the order of the first day (hammer and plates, mortar and dusts, bronze, coke oven, Nether sulfur with a lighter, surface stones that give away the vein) and the idea of asking for each intermediate in the chain | GregTech Community Pack Modern (GregTechCEu/GregTech-Modern-Community-Pack), `config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/` (*This Rocks!*, *S is for Sulfur*, steam and LV chapters) | LGPL-2.1 | Order and data, checked against the GTM 7.5.3 dump; new texts |
| EV chapter: the EV Circuit Assembler as the step before nano circuits, the nano chain asked task by task, NOR chips from the phosphorus boule, RTM coils as an EV goal, iridium and osmium kept for IV, and the warning that cryogenic fluids need the right pipes | GregTech Community Pack Modern (GregTechCEu/GregTech-Modern-Community-Pack), `config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ev__extreme_voltage.snbt` (*EV Circuit Assembling*, *Nano CPUs*, *NOR Gates*, *RTM Alloy Coils*, *Iridium, Osmium, and EV*, *This isn't Cryotheum!*) | LGPL-2.1 | Order and quest selection, checked against our dump; new texts |

| Spine and branches of the starting chapter, small restock rewards | Monifactory, `config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/groundwork.snbt` | LGPL-3.0 | Structure |
| Tools that do not break at the start, spine in one row with branches above and below, one-line warnings about what breaks playthroughs | GregTech Leisure (the user's instance) | No clear licence | Ideas only (`docs/quests.md`, section 12) |
